# Historical result index

All artifacts are retained. `aborted/` holds unfinished collectors and setup-only runs; `invalid/` holds empty/truncated artifacts or recorded correctness defects. Completed collections with app errors retain their raw measurements here and list the error below. Noise, low counts and a missing supplementary report are not grounds for moving a completed run.

Dates marked “recorded” come from the first introducing Git commit, not a recovered VM wall clock. Raw timestamps and measured values are unchanged. A directory mapping in [docs/MOVED.md](../../docs/MOVED.md) applies to all descendants.
